## Dependency and integration order

1. Start from actual main7a5e383, so its seven local commits are included once.
2. Merge complete approved audio-dashboard-v2: coherent APIs/contracts/schema before their consumers. A patch-equivalent commit remains historical provenance, not duplicated source logic.
3. Merge IELTS beta planning docs; no runtime or release authority follows.
4. Merge complete LiquidGlass history and reconcile JSX/styles with new feature behavior.
5. Reconcile original snapshot per file; preserve inline feedback per direct human choice. Never import audio worktree dirty extensions without separate scope.
6. Run candidate gates, one independent review, exact handoff. Deferred branches remain intact.

Other worktree dirty states are recorded in JSON, not transplanted. Audio worktree has additional uncommitted voice-preview/narration/bug-mandate work not covered by the approved committed-head integration. No source is discarded or altered.
